PSO BLUE BURST ULTIMATE DROP DATABASE
=====================================

PURPOSE
Complete packaged Blue Burst drop rows from the uploaded PSO Expert pack. Keep separate from GameCube/Xbox. The packaged BB dataset is Ultimate-only.

SOURCE BASIS
Blue Burst frontend drop database packaged in pso-blueburst-drops-ultimate.json.

RETRIEVAL RULE
Treat explicit platform labels, verification/status fields, source labels, and warnings as part of the fact. Never silently remove them.
